public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {

                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;

                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

```java
public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;

        return true;
    }

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;

        return true;
    }

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires vec != null;
    //@ requires \valid(vec);
    //@ requires \forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; vec[i] >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && vec[i] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != null && \valid(\result);
    //@ ensures \result.length == vec.length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; \result[i] == saturate((long)vec[i] * (long)a));
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            ret[i] = saturate((long) vec[i] * (long) a);
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \old(prod) > Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result == Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \old(prod) < Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    //@ ensures \result != Integer.MAX_VALUE && \result != Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result == (int)prod;
    private static int saturate(long prod) {
        if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
            return Integer.MIN_VALUE;
        } else {
            return (int) prod;
        }
    }
}
